# Bounded Cargo Planning And Shared Source Revision — Current Freeze

Root Source/ordinary checkpoint; goal and ticket remain active. All31 Root collectors below are terminal. No Cargo/rustc/metadata/compiler/provider/Native image, network/browser/SDK activation or physical cache retirement ran. No Git mutation or worktree. All Bun parents/children are born BUN_RUNTIME_TRANSPILER_CACHE_PATH=0 and NODE_DISABLE_COMPILE_CACHE=1.

## Consumed Reader And Fixed Work Bounds

The actual default selected Rust producer still consumes captureSelectedCargoConfigurationV1 and partitions complete raw planning freshness from the two actual code.program/code.receipt compiler-defining inputs. It preserves all14 existing effective compiler closure/invalidation cases, including unrelated lock/profile/manifest observations that change planning freshness without recompiling unrelated semantic units. This is actual Source behavior and fixture data, not an installed Cargo/Nx build result.

Configuration entry authenticates the genuine issued phase and cancellation before request DATA or filesystem access. It synchronously captures only closed plain own coordinates and exactly two dense unique defining names before its first await. Proxy/getter/symbol/hidden/sparse/extended/duplicate/escaping/copied-control/cancelled cases refuse without executing DATA. A10000-extra-key request now refuses its key count before building a descriptor-map object; this avoids the additional unbounded descriptor-map allocation, not all JS-engine key enumeration work. Coordinate strings must be canonical absolute paths under the workspace; defining names pass the actual shared exact Source inventory factory.

Schema-first configuration limits are256 planning names,64 ancestors,4194304 bytes per planning file,8388608 per defining file and67108864 actual aggregate bytes. A repeated planning/defining file keeps the stricter role. Before each actual streamed read, the consumer passes min(role limit, aggregate remaining) into the existing exact Source reader. Its fresh/cached reducing bound, physical ancestry/single-link/no-follow opened identity, EOF, accepted work, cancellation and closure remain defining lower behavior. Missing required files fail. No raw planning body is retained by this capture; results are frozen digest/length DATA. The defining code owner retains its own stricter receipt budget. B's broader general Source/JSON policy is unchanged.

## Shared Bounded Revision Primitive

The new lower inputs/revision component is pure Node MJS with a first-party declaration/schema and no top-level await or runtime JSON import. It permits only a canonical primitive absolute path≤256UTF16 units and a safe-integer reducing extent≤8388608. Retained UTF8 text also requires requested limit≤65536; fixed windows are≤65536. It captures physical directory ancestry≤64, refuses ancestor/leaf symlinks and multi-link/special leaves, binds lstat to the opened descriptor, checks opened stamps per chunk, checks full parents/path/descriptor before and after streaming and exact EOF, zeroes temporary buffers, attempts actual close and preserves the first Error/undefined over simultaneous close failure.

Its own known Source guard uses the same bounded capture, with reload extent reduced to initially captured length. These are ordinary Source observations; returned sha256/byteLength/text grants no Native/store/admission authority. The host JS module loader's initial allocation and exact executing-byte custody are outside this proof. A known-hash check is not an exact executing-byte loader proof. Fresh Node execution of the current helper is recorded separately; the TypeScript configuration positives are Bun Source.

CFG has no runtime schema JSON import or readFileSync. It privately parses schema text retained from the same bounded opened descriptor≤64KiB, captures its own Source≤8MiB, and compares both current known revisions at entry/delivery using the smaller initially captured lengths. Six actual-body oversized Source/schema cases (entry, delivery, bootstrap) observe zero raw reads and zero target opens. These negative laws use a pre-import ordinary FS observation port with sparse ticket fixtures, never a physical Source replacement or accepted Native/provider substitution.

Compiler lane separately consumes this helper in S/B/O/E/R/DATA loader and actual Graph authority/compact/inference rosters. That shared integration is a different freeze and remains independently attributed. The lower helper and its declaration/schema are stable Root-owned Source here.

## Executed Evidence And Corrections

The four registered cargo-planning-source test/types prototype/release launches run these exact routes. No new executable command, budget or role alias was introduced for the helper.

Neutral corpora contain40 CFG vectors (7reducing reads,6planning extents,15own requests,2actual aggregate boundaries,4known drift and6guard extents) plus20 lower revision extent/physical vectors. Existing14 Cargo/TOML/SQLite mutation cases remain. Ajv validates contracts; SQLite independently models admission and dependency reachability; independent Bun SHA validates the Node crypto helper output. Sparse actual64MiB aggregate succeeds exactly and one extra byte refuses before the next descriptor read. The child filesystem fixtures are ordinary generated ticket data, not admitted images or accepted cache stores.

Ten lower physical/failure children cover actual ancestor/leaf symlink/hardlink refusal, actual file and parent replacement, exact Error/undefined plus close failure, close-only failure, shortened read and extra EOF. Timed read/close faults and two EOF faults use an ordinary FS test port; this is actual current-body consumption, not a general physical fault-recovery claim. Every child is terminal; healthy parent Original remains live. Actual attempted descriptors close exactly once in the tested opened cases.

Initial guard-red1 did not reach the guard because its preceding64MiB body exhausted the unchanged25-second sdk-fence deadline. The earlier green5 similarly cancelled a two-case aggregate body; cases were then registered separately before work, with the same standard budget/shared file Original and unchanged total command budget. No failed Original, clock or budget was healed. Final phases retain all assertions. Green4's raw FS reassignment did not intercept Bun's captured named import; it is fixture preflight failure, not a proven stale guard regression. Corrected pre-import Source port reaches the actual body.

Independent LOW found the real P2 raw readFileSync epoch allocation. Guard-red2 actually reports rawReads1 before refusal; the new capture reports zero. Wide-red1 reaches descriptor-map count1 before the early key-count fix. The shared primitive initially accepted a real parent symlink; ancestry-red reproduces status0 instead of refusal before the defining ancestry correction. Its missing-module Node RED and both ordinary Node positives remain. Types4 failed only an in-flight Native mapping type import subsequently fixed by its owner. All these failures/cascades are preserved and distinguished from final gates.

## Scope And Concurrent Source Identity

The before/after bounded snapshots cover1683 observed static-relative Source paths plus predecessor observations, not a complete executing/runtime closure. All12 Root-changed files matched across final gates. One observed Graph file changed during the gates: its exact before/current identities are below. Compiler announced that G-only private policy correction and separately validates it; this Root freeze does not claim zero whole-closure drift or qualify the newer Graph as a runtime positive. Current review Source identities are listed, with the changed Graph row explicitly current-only.

Predecessor11/467 BOTH/five-root strict checkpoints predate the independent P2 correction and are not final clean evidence. Original selected producer still memoizes configuration per distinct manifest/workspace/cwd, so shared bytes may be rehashed across different crate manifests. B still performs its existing full ancestry work per async chunk; the performance cut needs separate race laws and is not silently applied here. This freeze claims bounded read work/retention, not fast whole builds.

Native/cache physical size enforcement, sustainable mandatory Graph retirement/epoch invalidation, per-context actual Rust producer concurrency, cross-platform effects, installed Nx/Cargo current cold/warm/restore/all-core/multiple-agent behavior, default SDK/publication and full Source-to-promotion interval remain OPEN/HOLD. Managed64GiB is not a whole repository/Git/ticket/historical-cache bound. Oversized facade crates/lock graph issues remain separately tracked. U descriptor/receipt raw-read and reader-order findings are the next coordinated cut; compact DAG publication is design only.
